1	// 提取墙面物品映射：Item.cs SetDefaults1-5 的 `case N:` 块内 `createWall = M;`
2	// → src/data/vanilla-wallitems.json（itemId → createWall）
3	import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'fs';
4	
5	const itemSrc = readFileSync('~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs', 'utf8');
6	const vanilla = JSON.parse(readFileSync('public/sprites/vanilla.json', 'utf8'));
7	
8	const body = itemSrc.slice(itemSrc.indexOf('public void SetDefaults1'), itemSrc.indexOf('public void SetDefaults(int Type'));
9	const caseRe = /\t\tcase (\d+):\n/g;
10	const marks = [];
11	let m;
12	while ((m = caseRe.exec(body))) marks.push({ id: Number(m[1]), at: m.index });
13	const map = {};
14	for (let i = 0; i < marks.length; i++) {
15	  const end = i + 1 < marks.length ? marks[i + 1].at : marks[i].at + 4000;
16	  const seg = body.slice(marks[i].at, end);
17	  const cw = seg.match(/createWall = (\d+);/);
18	  if (cw) map[marks[i].id] = Number(cw[1]);
19	}
20	// 名字（vanilla.json items.key）→ slug
21	const slug = (k) => k.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, '').toLowerCase();
22	const out = {};
23	for (const [id, wall] of Object.entries(map)) {
24	  const name = vanilla.items[id]?.key ?? `Item${id}`;
25	  out[id] = { wall, key: `vi_${id}_${slug(name)}`, name };
26	}
27	writeFileSync('src/data/vanilla-wallitems.json', JSON.stringify(out));
28	console.log('wall items:', Object.keys(out).length, '| walls covered:', new Set(Object.values(out).map((v) => v.wall)).size);

Now let me understand the current state. The extractor:
1. Slices Item.cs from `SetDefaults1` to `SetDefaults(int Type`
2. Finds `\t\tcase (\d+):\n` marks
3. For each case segment, matches `createWall = (\d+);`

This misses:
1. **Shared case segments**: e.g. `case 2: case 29: case 1905: ... goto/fallthrough` — actually in C# decompiled code, cases like `case 2: case 29:` share the same block. The regex `\t\tcase (\d+):\n` requires the case to be followed by a newline, so `case 2: case 29:` on the same line won't match. Actually wait — let's think. `case 2: case 29:` — the regex requires `case (\d+):\n` so `case 2: case 29:` fails to match `case 2:` (since next char is space not newline). And `case 29:` at the end of the line would match if it ends the line... Hmm, but then the segment would start at the last case label.

2. **Expression assignments**: `createWall = wallId + N;` or `createWall = num++;` — e.g., in shared segment loops.

3. **DefaultToPlaceableWall helper**: items that call `DefaultsToPlaceableWall` or `DefaultToPlaceableWall(wall)`.

4. **`createWall = WallID.X;`** symbolic constants — regex only matches digits.

5. **SetDefaults(int Type)** main dispatcher: there may be `SetDefaults(int Type, ...)` that calls `SetDefaults1..5`. Also there may be a final switch in the main one.

6. **autoCreate / createWall via other paths**.

Let me grep the vanilla Item.cs for all `createWall` mentions to understand the scale.

		if (Type < 0)

			return;
		}
		ResetStats(Type);
		if (type >= ItemID.Count)
		{
			type = 0;
		}
		if (variant == null)

			variant = ItemVariants.SelectVariant(Type);

import wallItemsJson from './vanilla-wallitems.json';
for (const wi of Object.values(wallItemsJson as Record<string, { wall: number; key: string }>)) {
  const existing = byKey[wi.key];
  if (existing !== undefined) { ITEM_DEFS[existing].wallId = wi.wall; continue; }
  item({ key: wi.key, name: '', maxStack: 999, value: 1, wallId: wi.wall });

/** internal id → 稳定 id(保存方向;0=无映射,不应出现) */
export const ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL = new Uint16Array(ITEM_DEFS.length);
/** 稳定 id → internal id(加载方向;同 vid 重复注册时归一到首个 def——内容等价) */
export const ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE = new Map<number, number>();
for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const k = ITEM_DEFS[i].key;
  const vm = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(k);
  // place_v_N_* 未在 PRIV 表登记时按 sheet 派生 20000+N(确定性,与表内显式号不冲突:
  // 表尾已到 10500,20000+ 区段独占)——新注册 tile 的放置物不再逐个补表
  const pm = !vm ? /^place_v_(\d+)_/.exec(k) : null;
  const s = vm ? Number(vm[1]) : PRIV_ITEM_STABLE[k] ?? (pm ? 20000 + Number(pm[1]) : undefined);
  if (s === undefined) { console.error(`[stable-id] item 缺稳定 id: ${k}`); continue; }
  ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[i] = s;
  if (!ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.has(s)) ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.set(s, i);
}
// ---- 原版工具力表补齐(Item.cs SetDefaults 提取)→ vi_ 道具 tool 元数据 ----
// 条件破坏链的道具入口:圣锤 80 砸祭坛/邪恶石 65 需梦魇镐/神庙砖 210 需 Picksaw 级等
// ⚠ axe 单位=源码原值(斧力%÷5,如 Drax axe=22=110%)——消费端 int(axe*1.2);
//   曾误存 70(当全百分比)致双工具砍树快 3 倍,2026-08-13 对齐 Player.cs:45098
const VANILLA_TOOL_POWERS: Record<number, { pick?: number; hammer?: number; axe?: number }> = JSON.parse("{\"103\":{\"pick\":65},\"104\":{\"hammer\":55},\"122\":{\"pick\":100},\"204\":{\"hammer\":60,\"axe\":20},\"217\":{\"hammer\":70,\"axe\":30},\"367\":{\"hammer\":80},\"579\":{\"pick\":200,\"axe\":22},\"654\":{\"hammer\":40},\"657\":{\"hammer\":35},\"660\":{\"hammer\":55},\"776\":{\"pick\":110},\"777\":{\"pick\":150},\"778\":{\"pick\":180},\"787\":{\"hammer\":85},\"797\":{\"hammer\":55},\"798\":{\"pick\":70},\"922\":{\"hammer\":40},\"990\":{\"pick\":200,\"axe\":22},\"1188\":{\"pick\":130},\"1195\":{\"pick\":165},\"1202\":{\"pick\":190},\"1234\":{\"hammer\":90},\"1262\":{\"hammer\":90},\"1305\":{\"hammer\":100,\"axe\":35},\"1506\":{\"pick\":200},\"1507\":{\"hammer\":90,\"axe\":30},\"3481\":{\"hammer\":59},\"3487\":{\"hammer\":50},\"3493\":{\"hammer\":43},\"3499\":{\"hammer\":38},\"3505\":{\"hammer\":35},\"3511\":{\"hammer\":45},\"3517\":{\"hammer\":55},\"3525\":{\"hammer\":100,\"axe\":30},\"4317\":{\"hammer\":80}}");
for (const [idS, tp] of Object.entries(VANILLA_TOOL_POWERS)) {
  const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[Number(idS)];
  if (!key) continue;
  const def = ITEM_DEFS[ITEM_BY_KEY[key]];
  if (!def) continue;
  const type = tp.pick !== undefined ? 'pick' : 'hammer';
  def.tool = { type: type as ToolType, power: tp.pick ?? tp.hammer ?? 0 };
  if (tp.axe !== undefined) def.axePower = tp.axe;
}

Plan for the parser:
1. Region = from `public void SetDefaults1(int type)` to `public void SetDefaults(int Type, ItemVariant variant = null)`.
2. Find all case labels: `/^(\t+)case (\d+):\s*$/gm`. 
3. For each case label at depth d, the code body = from the label line end to the next label (`case N:` or `default:`) at depth <= d, or to a line `^(\t{0,d-1})}` (dedent below case depth) — but careful: the body can contain nested braces at deeper levels; a closing `}` at exactly depth d-? Hmm.

In ILSpy output with tabs, a switch at depth k (statement level) has its cases at indentation k tabs, and the body statements at k tabs too. The closing `}` of the switch is at k-1 tabs.

So: for a case label line at tab-depth `d`, its body spans until:
- next line matching `^\t{1,d}case \d+:` (same depth label) → new case
- or line matching `^\t{1,d}default:` → default
- or line matching `^\t{1,d-1}}` → close of switch (body ends)

Wait, but case body might contain nested blocks (if/switch) with their own cases at deeper depth — those deeper `case` labels have d+1 or more tabs, so they won't be confused. Good.

Also `default:` labels at depth d.

4. For `goto case N;` in a body: follow the target case body in the same switch (same depth d, in the same switch block). To resolve "same switch", I can search forward/backward for a case label at depth d with value N within the same switch. Simple approach: find the nearest case label with id N at the same depth d whose switch is the same. Since switches are separated by `}` at depth d-1... Let me handle: collect all case labels at depth d in order; a "switch group" at depth d is a maximal run of consecutive labels at depth d uninterrupted by a dedent below d. Then `goto case N` from a body resolves to case N within the same group.

The single goto case is at 30016: `goto case 3262;`. Let me look at its context to confirm the mechanism works.

Expression evaluation:
```js
function evalExpr(expr, type) {
  // strip (ushort) casts
  const e = expr.replace(/\(ushort\)/g, '').replace(/\(int\)/g, '').trim();
  if (!/^[-+*/() \d]|type/.test(...)) // validate charset
  const js = e.replace(/\btype\b/g, `(${type})`);
  // validate: only digits, ops, parens, spaces
  if (!/^[-+*/() 0-9]+$/.test(js)) throw new Error(`unsupported expr: ${expr}`);
  const v = Function(`"use strict";return (${js});`)();
  return Math.trunc(v);
}
```

```
// NPC 帧数金标硬闸门（2026-08-13）：json × Main.npcFrameCount[697] × 贴图 三方对账，
// 运行时直读反编译源码——零快照零腐烂，数据漂移必炸。
// 背景：668 鹿角怪曾误填 25、129/130 填 1、13 条整缺失（589 困境高尔夫球手被
// spawner spawnBound 静默丢弃从未出现）——全部由本闸门三类检查覆盖。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { VANILLA_NPCS } from '../src/data/vanillaNpcs';

const ROOT = fileURLToPath(new URL('../..', import.meta.url)); // game/ 的上一级=仓库根
const MAIN_CS = `${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Main.cs`;

/** 流式提取 Main.npcFrameCount[697]（Main.cs:65994 起的初始化数组：
 *  去 // 注释后从 '{' 收集到 '};'——已与 697 项全长核对） */
function vanillaFrameCounts(): number[] {
  const lines = readFileSync(MAIN_CS, 'utf8').split('\n');
  const start = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes('npcFrameCount = new int['));
  expect(start, 'Main.cs 中未找到 npcFrameCount 数组（反编译树路径变？）').toBeGreaterThan(0);
  const buf: string[] = [];
  for (let i = start; i < Math.min(lines.length, start + 400); i++) {
    buf.push(lines[i]);
    if (lines[i].includes('};')) break;
  }
  const blob = buf.join('\n');
  const body = blob.slice(blob.indexOf('{') + 1, blob.indexOf('};'));
  const nums = body.replace(/\/\/.*/g, '').match(/\d+/g);
  const vals = (nums ?? []).map(Number);
  expect(vals.length, '数组长度应 697').toBe(697);
  return vals;
}

```

i < lines.length; i++) {\n  if (!inRegion(i)) continue;\n  if (!/\\bcreateWall\\s*=|DefaultToPlaceableWall\\(/.test(lines[i])) continue;\n  const claimed = [...wallOf.values()].some((v) => v.line === i);\n  if (!claimed) unattributed.push(i + 1);\n}\nif (unattributed.length) {\n  throw new Error(`有 ${unattributed.length} 处赋值行未被归因（解析缺口）: 行 ${unattributed.join(',')}`);\n}\n\n// ---- 对账闸门 2：墙 id 必须落在 WallID 常量表内 ----\nconst wallConsts = new Map();\nfor (const m of readFileSync(WALLID_CS, 'utf8').matchAll(/public const ushort (\\w+) = (\\d+);/g)) {\n  wallConsts.set(Number(m[2]), m[1]);\n}\nconst unknownWalls = new Set();\nfor (const { wall } of wallOf.values()) {\n  if (wall <= 0 || !wallConsts.has(wall)) unknownWalls.add(wall);\n}\nif (unknownWalls.size) throw new Error(`墙 id 无 WallID 常量: ${[...unknownWalls].join(',')}`);\n\n// ---- 名字（vanilla.json items.key）→ 稳定 key，落盘 ----\nconst vanilla = JSON.parse(readFileSync('public/sprites/vanilla.json', 'utf8'));\nconst slug = (k) =>\n  k\n    .replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2')\n    .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '_')\n    .replace(/^_|_$/g, '')\n    .toLowerCase();\nconst out = {};\nfor (const id of [...wallOf.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)) {\n  const { wall, line } = wallOf.get(id);\n  const name = vanilla.items[id]?.key ?? `Item${id}`;\n  out[id] = { wall, key: `vi_${id}_${slug(name)}`, name, line: line + 1 };\n}\nwriteFileSync('src/data/vanilla-wallitems.json', JSON.stringify(out));\n\n// ---- 报告 ----\nconst prev = JSON.parse(readFileSync('src/data/vanilla-wallitems.json', 'utf8'));\nconsole.log(`wall items: ${Object.keys(out).length} | walls covered: ${new Set(Object.values(out).map((v) => v.wall)).size}`);\nconsole.log(`(对账闸门: ${unattributed.length} 处未归因, ${unknownWalls.size} 个未知墙 id)`);\nconsole.log(`(items.ts 消费侧共 ${Object.keys(prev).length} 条)`);\n"

So: among labels at depth k-1 with line < L, take the latest one such that all lines between its group's end and L have depth > k-1. Simpler: iterate labels at depth k-1 in reverse order from L; the first one found where the segment from (that label's line+1) to L has no line with depth <= k-1 → that's the enclosing group's last label; then extend backward over adjacent label lines to get the whole run.

key: string; name: string; line: number };\nconst table = wallItems as Record<string, Entry>;\n\nconst itemLines = readFileSync(ITEM_CS, 'utf8').split('\\n');\nconst depthOf = (i: number): number => {\n  let d = 0;\n  while (itemLines[i]?.[d] === '\\t') d++;\n  return d;\n};\n\n/** SetDefaults1-5 方法体切片（按下一方法声明收边；:27625 DefaultToQuestFish、\n *  :35420 DefaultToGolfBall 夹在中间须排除，DefaultToPlaceableWall 定义 :48017 在外） */\nfunction regionSlices(): Array<[number, number]> {\n  const decl = /^\\t(?:public|private|internal|protected)[\\w<>\\[\\], .]* [\\w]+\\(/;\n  const out: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n  for (const n of [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]) {\n    const s = itemLines.findIndex((l) => l.startsWith(`\\tpublic void SetDefaults${n}(`));\n    expect(s, `Item.cs 缺 SetDefaults${n}`).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    let e = s + 1;\n    while (e < itemLines.length && !decl.test(itemLines[e])) e++;\n    out.push([s + 1, e]);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\nconst SLICES = regionSlices();\nconst inRegion = (i: number) => SLICES.some(([a, b]) => i >= a && i < b);\n\nconst evalExpr = (expr: string, type: number): number => {\n  const js = expr.replace(/\\(ushort\\)/g, '').replace(/\\(int\\)/g, '').replace(/\\btype\\b/g, `(${type})`).replace(/\\s+/g, '');\n  expect(js, `不支持的 createWall 表达式: ${expr}`).toMatch(/^[-+*/()0-9]+$/);\n  return Math.trunc(Function(`\"use strict\"; return (${js});`)() as number);\n};\n\n/** 层 A：向上归因。每个赋值行 → 归属物品 id 集合。\n *  规则：赋值行缩进 k，归属 = 缩进 k-1 的 case 标签连排组（内层 switch 的 case 恰好\n *  在 k-1，天然取到最内层）；无标签则回退到最小包含 if(type…) 区间段。 */\nfunction upwardLedger(): Map<number, { wall: number; line: number }> {\n  const labelRe = /^(\\t*)(case (\\d+)|default):$/;\n  const labels: Array<{ i: number; d: number; ids: number[] | null }> = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < itemLines.length; i++) {\n    if (!inRegion(i)) continue;\n    const m = labelRe.exec(itemLines[i]);\n    if (m) labels.push({ i, d: m[1].length, ids: m[3] ? [Number(m[3])] : null });\n  }\n  const rangeRe = /if \\(type (>=|>|<=|<) (\\d+) && type (>=|>|<=|<) (\\d+)\\)/;\n  const cmp = (op: string, a: number, b: number) =>\n    op === '>=' ? a >= b : op === '>' ? a > b : op === '<=' ? a <= b : a < b;\n  // 全体 if(type…) 区间段及其花括号块行界\n  const ranges: Array<{ from: number; to: number; lo: number; hi: number; ops: [string, string] }> = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < itemLines.length; i++) {\n    if (!inRegion(i)) continue;\n    const m = rangeRe.exec(itemLines[i]);\n    if (!m) continue;\n    let net = 0;\n    let started = false;\n    let j = i;\n    do {\n      net += (itemLines[j].match(/\\{/g) ?? []).length - (itemLines[j].match(/\\}/g) ?? []).length;\n      if (net > 0) started = true;\n      j++;\n    } while (j < itemLines.length && (!started || net > 0));\n    const [x, y] = [Number(m[2]), Number(m[4])].sort((p, q) => p - q);\n    ranges.push({ from: i, to: j, lo: x, hi: y, ops: [m[1], m[3]] });\n  }\n\n  const ledger = new Map<number, { wall: number; line: number }>();\n  let occurrences = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < itemLines.length; i++) {\n    if (!inRegion(i)) continue;\n    const cw = /\\bcreateWall\\s*=\\s*([^;]+);/.exec(itemLines[i]);\n    const dw = /DefaultToPlaceableWall\\((.+?)\\);/.exec(itemLines[i]);\n    if (!cw && !dw) continue;\n    occurrences++;\n    const expr = (cw ? cw[1] : dw![1]).trim();\n    const k = depthOf(i);\n    // (a) 最靠近的上一行缩进 k-1 标签，且其与赋值行之间无缩进 <= k-1 的行\n    let groupEnd = -1;\n    for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0; j--) {\n      if (depthOf(j) < k) { groupEnd = j; break; } // switch 收口或更外层 → 不属任何 case 体\n      if (depthOf(j) === k - 1 && labelRe.test(itemLines[j])) { groupEnd = j; break; }\n    }\n    let owners: number[] = [];\n    if (groupEnd >= 0 && labelRe.test(itemLines[groupEnd]) && depthOf(groupEnd) === k - 1) {\n      // 沿相邻同缩进标签行向左扩成连排组\n      let j = groupEnd;\n      while (j - 1 >= 0 && labelRe.test(itemLines[j - 1]) && depthOf(j - 1) === k - 1 && itemLines[j - 1].endsWith(':')) j--;\n      for (let t = j; t <= groupEnd; t++) {\n        const m = labelRe.exec(itemLines[t]);\n        if (m?.[3]) owners.push(Number(m[3]));\n      }\n    } else {\n      // (b) 区间段：最小包含块\n      let best: (typeof ranges)[number] | undefined;\n      for (const r of ranges) if (i >= r.from && i < r.to && (!best || r.to - r.from < best.to - best.from)) best = r;\n      expect(best, `Item.cs:${i + 1} createWall 既不在 case 体也不在 if(type…) 区间段`).toBeTruthy();\n      for (let id = best!.lo; id <= best!.hi; id++) {\n        if (cmp(best!.ops[0], id, Number(rangeRe.exec(itemLines[best!.from])![2])) &&\n            cmp(best!.ops[1], id, Number(rangeRe.exec(itemLines[best!.from])![4]))) owners.push(id);\n      }\n    }\n    expect(owners.length, `Item.cs:${i + 1} createWall 归属为空`).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    for (const id of owners) {\n      const wall = evalExpr(expr, id);\n      const prev = ledger.get(id);\n      if (prev) expect(prev.wall, `item ${id} 两处 createWall 不一致(:${prev.line + 1} vs :${i + 1})`).toBe(wall);\n      else ledger.set(id, { wall, line: i });\n    }\n  }\n  expect(occurrences, '源码侧 createWall 赋值行数').toBeGreaterThan(200);\n  return ledger;\n}\n\ndescribe('wallitems parity（Item.cs createWall 全量 ↔ vanilla-wallitems.json）', () => {\n  const ledger = upwardLedger();\n\n  it('A. 表与源码账本逐条相等（id 集合 + 墙 id 值）', () => {\n    const tableIds = new Set(Object.keys(table).map(Number));\n    const ledgerIds = new Set(ledger.keys());\n    expect([...tableIds].filter((id) => !ledgerIds.has(id)), '表内有、源码无的条目').toEqual([]);\n    expect([...ledgerIds].filter((id) => !tableIds.has(id)), '源码有、表内漏的条目（放置静默无效根因）').toEqual([]);\n    for (const [id, { wall }] of ledger) expect(table[String(id)].wall, `item ${id}`).toBe(wall);\n  });\n\n  it('A2. 条目数与墙覆盖量（2026-08-13 基线 292/292，表扩张须同步本断言）', () => {\n    expect(Object.keys(table).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(292);\n    expect(new Set(Object.values(table).map((e) => e.wall)).size).toBe(Object.keys(table).length);\n  });\n\n  it('B. 表结构：wall 正整数 / key=vi_{id}_{slug(name)} / name 对齐 vanilla.json / line 指向赋值行', () => {\n    const vanilla = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/public/sprites/vanilla.json`, 'utf8')).items;\n    const slug = (k: string) =>\n      k.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, '').toLowerCase();\n    for (const [idS, e] of Object.entries(table)) {\n      const id = Number(idS);\n      expect(Number.isInteger(e.wall) && e.wall > 0, `item ${id} wall 非法`).toBe(true);\n      expect(e.key, `item ${id} key`).toBe(`vi_${id}_${slug(e.name)}`);\n      expect(vanilla[idS]?.key, `item ${id} name 与 vanilla.json 不符`).toBe(e.name);\n      expect(/\\bcreateWall\\s*=|DefaultToPlaceableWall\\(/.test(itemLines[e.line - 1] ?? ''), `item ${id} line ${e.line} 不是赋值行`).toBe(true);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('C. WallID.cs 常量语义对账（墙 id 有专名，物品名/墙名规范化后相等或命中原版命名怪癖白名单）', () => {\n    const consts = new Map<number, string>();\n    for (const m of readFileSync(WALLID_CS, 'utf8').matchAll(/public const ushort (\\w+) = (\\d+);/g)) {\n      consts.set(Number(m[2]), m[1]);\n    }\n    const slug = (k: string) =>\n      k.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, '_').replace(/^_|_$/g, '').toLowerCase();\n    // 原版命名怪癖：地牢三色 Brick↔Dungeon、块墙省 Block、灰浆 Gray、原版拼写错\n    // RichMaogany、RainbowStainedGlass、MetalFence、Gray/Grey、ArcaneRunes、Echo/Unsafe 族、\n    // LihzahrdBrick、Shimmerfall 等（ItemID 名与 WallID 名不同字面但同物）\n    const QUIRKS = new Set([\n      '135', '138', '140', '618', '623', '768', '769', '770', '1272', '1378', '1379', '1380', '1381',\n      '1382', '1383', '1448', '2008', '2271', '3238', '3273', '3340', '3341', '3342', '3343', '3344',\n      '3345', '3346', '3348', '5365', '5366', '5367', '5368', '5369', '5370', '5371', '5372', '5373',\n      '5374', '5375', '5376', '5397', '5418', '5420', '5422', '5494',\n    ]);\n    for (const [idS, e] of Object.entries(table)) {\n      const wname = consts.get(e.wall);\n      expect(wname, `item ${idS} 墙 id ${e.wall} 无 WallID.cs 常量`).toBeTruthy();\n      const a = slug(e.name);\n      const b = slug(wname!);\n      const ok = a === b || a === `${b}_wall` || a.replace(/_wall$/, '') === b || QUIRKS.has(idS);\n      expect(ok, `item ${idS}(${e.name}) → 墙 ${e.wall}(${wname}) 命名对不上且不在怪癖白名单`).toBe(true);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('D. 缺口清单 §2 十四件逐件定案：6 真墙物品入表、8 假阳性（扫表器误报）以源码行号证伪', () => {\n    // 真：共享 case 段算式（:21113/:21402/:22064/:22344/:24059/:26624/:33303）\n    expect(table['1957']?.wall).toBe(125); // Grinch Finger Wallpaper  :21113 116+type-1948\n    expect(table['2014']?.wall).toBe(132); // Starlit Heaven Wallpaper  :21402 126+type-2008\n    expect(table['2160']?.wall).toBe(135); // Ducky Wallpaper           :22064 133+type-2158\n    expect(table['2213']?.wall).toBe(141); // Shadewood Fence           :22344 138+type-2210\n    expect(table['2434']?.wall).toBe(148); // Sail                      :24059 146+type-2432\n    expect(table['2791']?.wall).toBe(175); // Shroomite Plating Wall    :26624 173+type-2789\n    expect(table['3762']?.wall).toBe(230); // Silly Green Balloon Wall  :33303 228+(type-3760)\n    // 假阳性：源码侧无 createWall——放置链各自另有机制\n    expect(table['2']).toBeUndefined();      // Dirt Block → createTile=0（:1390）\n    expect(table['29']).toBeUndefined();     // Life Crystal（:1706 无放置）\n    expect(table['206']).toBeUndefined();    // Water Bucket（:3960 倾倒液体非放置）\n    expect(table['207']).toBeUndefined();    // Lava Bucket（:3969 同上）\n    expect(table['215']).toBeUndefined();    // Whoopie Cushion（:4030 坐垫音效）\n    expect(table['1124']).toBeUndefined();   // Hive = tileWand 消耗材（:13869 tileWand=1124）\n    expect(table['1905']).toBeUndefined();   // Blue and Yellow Lights = 圣诞树顶饰\n                                             //   （Player.cs:41055 PlaceThing_XMasTreeTops 1874-1905）\n    expect(table['2262']).toBeUndefined();   // Blue Dynasty Shingles → createTile=313（:22752）\n                                             //   墙是 2263/2264（:22768 区间段）\n  });\n\n  it('E. 三种写法锚点：直赋 / DefaultToPlaceableWall 辅助 / if 区间段 / 嵌套 switch 内层 case', () => {\n    expect(table['26']?.wall).toBe(1);      // Stone Wall 直赋 :1675\n    expect(table['5365']?.wall).toBe(7);    // Cursed Blue Brick Wall DefaultToPlaceableWall(7) :42537\n    expect(table['2263']?.wall).toBe(142);  // White Dynasty Wall 区间段 :22764-22769\n    expect(table['2264']?.wall).toBe(143);  // Blue Dynasty Wall  同段\n    expect(table['4486']?.wall).toBe(246);  // Ebonstone Wall DefaultToPlaceableWall((ushort)(246+type-4486)) :37858\n    expect(table['2678']?.wall).toBe(157);  // Offline Amber Gemspark Wall 嵌套 switch 内层 :25888\n    expect(table['2690']?.wall).toBe(163); ## 报告:墙物品放置链全量扩充(功能缺口 §2)

### 提取方法(行号锚,全部以 1456 反编译为准)
重写 `game/tools/extract-wallitems.mjs`:旧版只认 `\t\tcase N:\n + createWall = 数字;` 一种写法。新版做结构化解析,覆盖全部写法(先例 = items.ts CAGE_ITEM_SHEET 头注"三种写法"):
- **直赋**:`createWall = N;`(:1675 Stone Wall)、`DefaultToPlaceableWall(N);`(:42537,辅助定义 :48017)
- **共享 case 段算式**:`case 1949:…case 1957:` 连排共享一体,`createWall = 116 + type - 1948;`(:21113);同类 :21402/:22064/:22344/:24059/:26624/:33303
- **if 区间段**:`if (type >= 2263 && type <= 2264) { createWall = 142 + type - 2263; }`(:22764-22769)
- **DefaultToPlaceableWall 区间**:`(ushort)(246 + type - 4486)`(:37858,4486-4540 → 246-303)
- **结构陷阱**:①嵌套 switch(SetDefaults3/4/5 的 default 链逐段下沉,case 缩进 3-5 层)——扫外层 case 体时整块跳过内层 switch,否则 2677-2690 宝石火花墙 14 处赋值会被外层误吞(:25866-25929);②`goto case 3262`(:30016,全区域唯一)跟随目标并入;③方法体按下一方法声明收边,排除夹在中间的 DefaultToQuestFish(:27625)/DefaultToGolfBall(:35420)。全区域 121 个 switch 全部 `switch (type)`,case 标签直映物品 id。

内置两道闸门:每处赋值行必须归因 ≥1 件(**未归因 0**)、每个墙 id 必须有 WallID.cs 常量(**未知 0**)。脚本幂等,重跑 0 增 0 删 0 改。

### 新增条目数
`vanilla-wallitems.json` **124 → 292 条(+168,0 更正 0 删除)**,292 条 ↔ 292 个墙 id 一一双射。每条带 `line` 字段(源码行号锚,如 1957 → :21113)。

### 覆盖对账(tests/wallitems-parity.test.ts,7/7 绿)
- **A 层**:测试内用**独立的向上归因算法**(每处赋值行从下往上找归属 case 组/区间段)重推导,与提取器的"标签→体"向下解析互为独立实现,逐条相等——共享解析 bug 必炸
- **C 层**:WallID.cs 367 常量语义对账,物品名/墙名规范化后全对上(45 条原版命名怪癖白名单:Dungeon↔Brick、Echo/Unsafe 族、RichMaogany 原版拼写错等)
- **缺口清单 §2 十五件逐件定案**:真墙物品 7 件入表——1957→125、2014→132、2160→135、2213→141、2434→148、2791→175、3762→230;**8 件为扫表器假阳性**(源码行号证伪):2 泥土块 createTile=0(:1390)、29 生命水晶(:1706)、206/207 水桶/熔岩桶(倾倒液体)、215 坐垫(:4030)、1124 蜂巢块=tileWand 消耗材(:13869)、1905 圣诞树顶饰(Player.cs:41055 PlaceThing_XMasTreeTops)、2262 蓝王朝瓦=createTile 313(:22752,墙是 2263/2264)

### 消费点与接线结论
消费点在 `src/data/items.ts:1252-1258`(全表遍历,已注册键补 wallId、未注册的按 vi_ 自动注册)——**纯数据驱动,Game.ts 零接线**。运行时放置链 Game.ts:4720/5311/7752 只判 `heldDef.wallId != null`,新条目自动生效;测试 F 层已断言全表 292 键在 ITEM_BY_KEY 均解析且 wallId 正确。另核 `WALL_DEFS`(tiles.ts:168)367 墙全覆盖(缺 0),渲染/小地图同无缺口。
